Entries on this page are generated automatically from [OpenAPI specification](/api_reference/overview) diffs when releases ship. Breaking changes are reviewed by a human before publication. See [API Versioning](/api_reference/versioning) for what counts as a breaking change and how deprecations are announced. Endpoint paths are shown relative to the API base URL (`https://openrouter.ai/api/v1`).

{/*
  Review convention: Keep exactly one Badge on each breaking-change bullet's first line.
  Replace the generated red badge with a gray outlined "No action needed" badge when appropriate.
  Do not remove or move it; regeneration restores the default badge on badge-less bullets.
*/}

{/* api-changelog:entries */}

<Update label="October 9, 2026" tags={["API Keys","Breaking","End Users","Guardrails","Interns","Schemas"]} rss="12 breaking changes, 10 modified endpoints, 2 new response codes, 3 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `CreateEndUserRequest`: maxLength `256` modified<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. The maximum end-user ID length increased from 256 to 512 characters.
  - Used by [`POST /end-users`](/api/api-reference/end-users/register-an-end-user)
- `CreateEndUserRequest`: pattern `^[^\p{Cc}]+$` modified<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. The pattern now permits control characters other than NUL.
  - Used by [`POST /end-users`](/api/api-reference/end-users/register-an-end-user)
- `EndUser`: maxLength `256` modified<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">

<Update label="October 6, 2026" tags={["API Keys","BYOK","Breaking","Schemas"]} rss="5 breaking changes, 8 modified endpoints, 1 new response code, 2 new schemas, 9 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `BYOKKey`: required `declared_region` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required for existing consumers.** Responses from BYOK endpoints now include `declared_region` (`"global"`, `"europe"`, `"us"`, or `null`) indicating the key's declared data region.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials)
    - [`GET /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/get-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`PATCH /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/update-a-byok-provider-credential)
    - [`POST /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/create-a-byok-provider-credential)
  </Expandable>
- [`GET /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/list-api-keys): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. API key responses now include `last_used_at`, an ISO 8601 UTC timestamp of the key's most recent recorded usage, or `null` if none has been recorded.
- [`POST /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/create-a-new-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`GET /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-a-single-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`PATCH /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/update-an-api-key): required `last_used_at` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>

- [`DELETE /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/delete-an-intern): requestBody `requestBody` property added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Regenerated SDK clients: pass `{}` when no acknowledgement is needed, or `{ acknowledge_workspace_loss: true }` when deleting the intern also requires acknowledging workspace loss. Raw HTTP clients may continue to omit the body.

## Modified endpoints

- [`GET /key`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-current-api-key): response schema updated
- [`POST /interns`](/api/api-reference/interns/create-an-intern): description updated; response schema updated
- [`POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/interns/stream-a-chat-completion-with-an-intern): description updated
- [`DELETE /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/delete-an-intern): description updated
- [`GET /interns`](/api/api-reference/interns/list-interns): new parameter `starting_after`
- [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination): description updated; response schema updated

## New response codes

- [`DELETE /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/delete-an-intern): now returns `400`
- [`DELETE /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/delete-an-intern): now returns `413`
- [`PATCH /byok/{id}`](/api/api-reference/byok/update-a-byok-provider-credential): now returns `403`

## New schemas

- `DeleteInternRequest`
  - Used by [`DELETE /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/delete-an-intern)

## Modified schemas

- `InternChatCompletionRequest`: description updated
  - Used by [`POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/interns/stream-a-chat-completion-with-an-intern)
- `CreateInternRequest`: description updated
  - Used by [`POST /interns`](/api/api-reference/interns/create-an-intern)
- `TokenExchangeRequest`: pattern property_added
  - Used by [`POST /oauth/token`](/api/api-reference/oauth/exchange-a-workload-identity-token)
- `InternChatCompletionChunk`: description updated
  - Used by [`POST /interns/{internId}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/interns/stream-a-chat-completion-with-an-intern)
- `UpdateInternRequest`: description updated; minLength property_added; pattern property_added
  - Used by [`PATCH /interns/{internId}`](/api/api-reference/interns/update-an-intern)
- `InternListResponse`: new property `next_cursor`
  - Used by [`GET /interns`](/api/api-reference/interns/list-interns)

</Update>

  - SDK and Terraform callers: rename the path argument from `id` to `workspace_ref` (`workspaceRef` in camelCase clients). The URL value itself is unchanged.
- `/workspaces/{id}/budgets/{interval}`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - SDK and Terraform callers: rename the path argument from `id` to `workspace_ref` (`workspaceRef` in camelCase clients). The URL value itself is unchanged.

## New endpoints

- [`GET /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/list-workspace-budgets): List workspace budgets
- [`GET /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/get-a-workspace-budget): Get a workspace budget
- [`PUT /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/create-or-update-a-workspace-budget): Create or update a workspace budget
- [`DELETE /workspaces/{workspace_ref}/budgets/{interval}`](/api/api-reference/workspaces/delete-a-workspace-budget): Delete a workspace budget

</Update>

<Update label="August 24, 2026" tags={["API Keys","Breaking","Containers","Schemas"]} rss="9 breaking changes, 6 new endpoints, 7 modified endpoints, 2 new schemas, 11 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `ContainerReferenceEnvironment`: maxLength `20` modified<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - No action required for existing consumers. The maximum container reference length increased from 20 to 40 characters.

  <Expandable title="6 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`POST /chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/chat/create-a-chat-completion)
    - [`POST /messages`](/api/api-reference/anthropic-messages/create-a-message)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/chat/completions`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-chat-completions-request-body)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/messages`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-messages-request-body)
    - [`POST /presets/{slug}/responses`](/api/api-reference/presets/create-a-preset-from-a-responses-request-body)
    - [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response)
  </Expandable>
- `/containers/{session_id}/files`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `/containers/{session_id}/files/{file_id}`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `/containers/{session_id}/files/{file_id}/content`: endpoint object removed<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Use the corresponding `/containers/{container_id}/...` endpoint and pass the canonical `container_id` returned by a bash or shell tool result instead of the original session ID.
- `ObservabilityDatadogDestination`: default `https://api.us5.datadoghq.com` modified<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - If your Datadog site is US5 and you relied on the omitted URL default, explicitly set `url` to `https://api.us5.datadoghq.com`. The new default is the US1 endpoint, `https://api.datadoghq.com`.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/list-observability-destinations)
    - [`GET /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/get-an-observability-destination)
    - [`PATCH /observability/destinations/{id}`](/api/api-reference/observability/update-an-observability-destination)
    - [`POST /observability/destinations`](/api/api-reference/observability/create-an-observability-destination)
  </Expandable>
- [`GET /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/list-api-keys): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`POST /keys`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/create-a-new-api-key): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Connect client-secret callers must include a non-empty `external_user` when creating a key. Management-key callers should continue to omit this Connect-only field.
- [`GET /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/get-a-single-api-key): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- [`PATCH /keys/{hash}`](/api/api-reference/api-keys/update-an-api-key): required `external_user` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>

## Breaking changes

- `BYOKKey`: type `string` modified<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - Allow `workspace_id` to be `null` in BYOK responses. A null value identifies a global credential that is usable across every workspace; do not substitute the default workspace.

  <Expandable title="4 endpoints using this schema">
    - [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials)

<Update label="July 29, 2026" tags={["Benchmarks","Breaking","Files","Schemas","Workspaces"]} rss="17 breaking changes, 12 modified endpoints, 14 new response codes, 13 new schemas, 16 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- `FileMetadata`: schema object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
- `FileDeleteResponse`: discriminator `discriminator` object added<Badge stroke color="red" icon="triangle-exclamation" className="changelog-status-badge">Action required</Badge>
  - HTTP clients: no change. Requests without a vendor SDK still get the OpenRouter shape (`_shape: "openrouter"`) with the same fields as before. Typed SDK clients: this response type is now a union discriminated by `_shape`. In the TypeScript SDK, the field is named `shape`, so check `shape === "openrouter"` before reading OpenRouter fields such as `id` and `type`.
  - Used by [`DELETE /files/{file_id}`](/api/api-reference/files/delete-a-file)
- `FileDeleteResponse`: properties `id` object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Used by [`DELETE /files/{file_id}`](/api/api-reference/files/delete-a-file)

## Breaking changes

- `FusionCallAnalysisInProgressEvent`: required `analyst_model` property added<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required for existing consumers.** `analyst_model` replaces `judge_model` in this streaming event; `judge_model` is retained as a deprecated alias that always carries the same value.
  - Used by [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response)
- $.tags: responses object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **No action required.** The `responses` OpenAPI tag was renamed to `Responses` for consistent Title Case across the API Reference. This is a documentation-grouping change only: `POST /api/v1/responses` and its schemas are unchanged, SDK namespaces are unchanged (`client.responses.send()` in TypeScript and Python, `Sdk.Responses.Send()` in Go), and endpoint documentation URLs are unchanged.

<Update label="July 25, 2026" tags={["BYOK","Breaking","Responses","Schemas"]} rss="2 breaking changes, 2 modified endpoints, 5 modified schemas.">

## Breaking changes

- [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response): tags `beta.responses` property removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - **Migration: no action for HTTP clients.** The Responses API is now GA. `beta.responses` was an OpenAPI grouping tag and SDK namespace, never a URL. `POST /api/v1/responses` and its request and response schemas are unchanged, so raw HTTP callers and OpenAI-compatible clients (including the OpenAI Agents SDK) need no changes. The endpoint now appears under **Responses** instead of **Beta responses** in the API Reference.
  - **Migration for SDK users: optional until sunset.** The canonical namespace is now `responses`, with `client.responses.send()` (TypeScript, Python) and `client.Responses.Send()` (Go). The `beta.responses` namespace, and TypeScript's `betaResponsesSend` standalone function, keep working as deprecated aliases; their removal will be announced here with a sunset date before it ships.
- $.tags: beta.responses object removed<Badge stroke color="gray" className="changelog-status-badge">No action needed</Badge>
  - Same change as above at the specification level: the `beta.responses` tag definition is gone from `$.tags`.

## Modified endpoints

- [`POST /responses`](/api/api-reference/responses/create-a-response): tags property_added
- [`GET /byok`](/api/api-reference/byok/list-byok-provider-credentials): parameter `provider` updated

  - Used by [`POST /messages`](/api/api-reference/anthropic-messages/create-a-message)
- `ProviderName`: enum property_added
